So in `runSelector`:
```ts
const argResults = meta.args.map(arg => {
  const argName = (arg as any)._atomicSelectorName
  if (argName) return this.callSelector(argName, state, props)
  return arg(state, props)
})
const { dirty, cause } = this.checkDirty(meta)
// non-local arg comparison
let extraCause = null
for (let i=0;i<meta.args.length;i++) {
  const argName = (meta.args[i] as any)._atomicSelectorName
  if (argName && !this.selectors.has(argName)) {
    if (!Object.is(meta.lastArgResults?.[i], argResults[i])) {
      extraCause = `selector:${argName}`
    }
  }
}
const finalDirty = dirty || extraCause
const finalCause = dirty ? cause : extraCause
```

And after computing, store `meta.lastArgResults = argResults.map(unwrap)`.

This handles connected values at whole-output granularity.

Now, **atomic updates and evaluation count**: each dirty selector evaluation increments `evaluations` once. Good.

Now let's also consider **`logic.selectorHealth()` topologicalOrder**. We compute from the graph of deps (first segment matching a local selector
name). Let's implement:
```ts
topologicalOrder() {
  const names = [...this.selectors.keys()]
  const indegree = new Map(names.map(n => [n, 0]))
  const adj = new Map(names.map(n => [n, []]))
  for (const meta of this.selectors.values()) {
    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
      const root = firstSegment(dep)
      if (this.selectors.has(root) && root !== meta.name) {
        adj.get(root).push(meta.name)
        indegree.set(meta.name, indegree.get(meta.name)+1)
      }
    }
  }
  // Kahn
  const queue = names.filter(n => indegree.get(n) === 0).sort()
  const order = []
  while (queue.length) {
    const n = queue.shift()
    order.push(n)
    for (const m of adj.get(n)) {
      indegree.set(m, indegree.get(m)-1)
      if (indegree.get(m) === 0) queue.push(m)
    }
  }
  if (order.length !== names.length) {
    // cycle (shouldn't happen), throw
    throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
  }
  return order
}
```

Wait, but some selector names may not be referenced in any deps; they start indegree 0. Some may have deps that aren't in this.selectors (state-only)
— those don't add indegree. Good.

Now, `selectorHealth` selectors map: include all engine.selectors. For each, dependencies sorted, dependents from reverse map, evaluations,
dirtyCause.

Now, **stable identity**: we key by name in `engine.selectors`. The `logic.pathString + name` can be used as an internal key if needed. The
requirement says "Ensure the association between a selector and its health metadata uses a stable identity (e.g., combining logic.pathString and the
selector's local name) that persists through Kea's internal build-time function wrapping." Our engine is per-logic (per built logic), and metadata
keyed by name. That persists through wrapping because args call by name. Good.

Now, let's also think about **props selectors**. In `selectors()`, the `propSelectors` proxy currently returns functions `() => target[prop]` (for
props). When passed as args to atomic selectors, they're plain functions; calling `arg(state, props)` returns prop value. Fine. No tracking of props.

Now, let's think about **`logic.selector` and rootSelector**. In atomic mode, `logic.selector` remains the plain pathSelector function. But
`logic.selector` is also used by `addSelectorAndValue` in reducers? No, we replace reducer selector creation. But `logic.selector` may be used by user
code directly; fine.

Now, **engine lifecycle and middleware notification**: When a logic with atomic selectors mounts, its engine is registered. The middleware iterates
`context.atomicEngines`. But `getAtomicEngine(logic)` is called during build, registering the engine even before mount. If store created before logic,
middleware exists; if store created after, also fine.

We need to make sure `resetContext` clears `atomicEngines`. We'll add to `openContext`.

Now, **lifecycle compatibility**: The task says "The new engine intercepts core lifecycle hooks; valid implementations must ensure that standard
plugin event ordering (e.g., afterMount) is not disrupted." This suggests perhaps implementing as a plugin with `beforeMount`/`afterMount` hooks. We
can implement engine setup entirely within core builders and store middleware, without adding plugin hooks, so lifecycle is untouched. That should
satisfy "not disrupted" — we don't modify mount logic at all. The statement "The new engine intercepts core lifecycle hooks" might be describing an
expected implementation pattern (i.e., they expect it to hook lifecycle), but our approach doesn't need to. As long as tests pass, fine.

Actually, let me reconsider: maybe the expected implementation is a plugin that hooks `afterMount` to subscribe to the store, and `beforeUnmount` to
unsubscribe. But our middleware approach doesn't touch lifecycle. The requirement "valid implementations must ensure that standard plugin event
ordering (e.g., afterMount) is not disrupted" is just a guard — they don't require us to use hooks. Fine.

Now, let's also handle **`useSelector` snapshot caching**: `useSyncExternalStore` calls `getSnapshot` repeatedly; our selector returns cached when not
dirty, so no recompute. Good.

Now, let's write the code. First, let's create the atomic-selectors module.

But before coding, let me consider the `diffPaths` more carefully, especially for maps/sets and ancestor version bumps.

`diffPaths(oldVal, newVal, base)`:
```ts
function diffPaths(oldVal, newVal, base): string[] {
  if (Object.is(oldVal, newVal)) return []
  if (oldVal === null || newVal === null || typeof oldVal !== 'object' || typeof newVal !== 'object') {
    return [base]
  }
  if (oldVal instanceof Map && newVal instanceof Map) {
    const res = []
    const keys = new Set([...oldVal.keys(), ...newVal.keys()])
    for (const k of keys) {
      if (!oldVal.has(k) || !newVal.has(k) || !Object.is(oldVal.get(k), newVal.get(k))) {
        res.push(joinPath(base, `map:${String(k)}`))
      } else if (oldVal.get(k) !== newVal.get(k)) {
        res.push(joinPath(base, `map:${String(k)}`))
      }
    }
    return res
  }
  if (oldVal instanceof Set && newVal instanceof Set) {
    const res = []
    for (const v of oldVal) {
      if (!newVal.has(v)) res.push(joinPath(base, `set:${String(v)}`))
    }
    for (const v of newVal) {
      if (!oldVal.has(v)) res.push(joinPath(base, `set:${String(v)}`))
    }
    return res
  }
  if (Array.isArray(oldVal) && Array.isArray(newVal)) {
    const res = []
    const len = Math.max(oldVal.length, newVal.length)
    for (let i=0;i<len;i++) {
      if (!Object.is(oldVal[i], newVal[i])) {
        res.push(joinPath(base, String(i)))
      } else if (typeof oldVal[i]==='object' && oldVal[i]!==null && typeof newVal[i]==='object' && newVal[i]!==null) {
        res.push(...diffPaths(oldVal[i], newVal[i], joinPath(base, String(i))))
      }
    }
    return res
  }
  // plain objects
  const res = []
  const keys = new Set([...Object.keys(oldVal), ...Object.keys(newVal)])
  for (const k of keys) {
    const ov = oldVal[k], nv = newVal[k]
    if (!Object.is(ov, nv)) {
      if (ov !== null && nv !== null && typeof ov === 'object' && typeof nv === 'object' && !(ov instanceof Map) && !(nv instanceof Map) && !(ov
instanceof Set) && !(nv instanceof Set)) {
        res.push(...diffPaths(ov, nv, joinPath(base, k)))
      } else {
        res.push(joinPath(base, k))
      }
    }
  }
  return res
}
```
Wait, for arrays: if `oldVal[i]` is an object and `newVal[i]` is same reference, Object.is true -> skip. If different object references but same leaf
values, we recurse to find leaf changes; if none, no dep recorded. That's correct: replacing an object with an equal object should not invalidate
selectors reading its leaves. But if a selector reads the whole object (dep `user`), then version of `user` should bump only if some leaf changed. If
no leaf changed (deep-equal), no bump — so selector reading whole `user` would not re-evaluate even though reference changed. Is that correct? If the
object is deep-equal, the selector's output (whole object) would be a new reference, so a component using it would want to re-render (since snapshot
changed). Hmm. But for our version-based granularity, if a selector returns the whole `user` object and the state replaces it with a deep-equal new
object, the selector's cached value is the **old** reference; its dep is `user`; no leaf versions bumped, so it won't re-evaluate, and React won't
re-render even though the state object changed. Is that a problem? In Redux, reducers should only create new references when something changed, so
deep-equal replacement is unusual. But it's possible (e.g., `{...state, age: sameAge}` would create new object but all leaf values same). A selector
reading `user.name` shouldn't re-evaluate (correct). A selector reading whole `user` should arguably re-evaluate because the object reference changed,
but since its leaves are same, the "meaningful" change is none. Fine-grained reactivity says only re-evaluate when accessed leaves change. So not
re-evaluating is consistent with the spec. I'll accept it.

For Map values: if map value is an object and changes internally but same key, we record `map:key` only (not deeper). That matches the spec:
dependency for Map key access is `map:key`. If a selector reads `data.get('a').name`, our map proxy would record `data.map:a` (when get is called) and
then if we return a proxy for the value, `.name` would record `data.map:a.name`. But the spec says for Map key access use `data.map:a`. Should nested
property read record `data.map:a.name`? The requirement says "for Map key access, `<reducer>.map:<key>` (e.g. `data.map:a`)". So reading
`data.get('a').name` would record `data.map:a` and maybe `data.map:a.name`; the health deps would show both? Let's decide: the map proxy returns a
tracked proxy for the value with basePath `data.map:a`. Accessing `.name` records `data.map:a.name`. That's more granular. The spec's examples just
show `data.map:a` for key access. To be safe, we can keep nested tracking (record both `data.map:a` on get and `data.map:a.name` on property access).
The health `dependencies` would list `data.map:a` and possibly `data.map:a.name`. That's fine. For invalidation, when `data.map:a` value changes, we
bump `data.map:a`; a selector depending on `data.map:a.name` would not see `data.map:a.name` bumped (since diff for map only records `data.map:a`, not
deeper). So if a selector reads `data.get('a').name`, and the value's `.name` changes, we'd bump only `data.map:a`, and the selector's dep
`data.map:a.name` would not change -> no re-eval. That's a bug for nested map value reads.

To handle this correctly, the map diff should recurse into values when both old and new have the key and both values are objects, recording
`data.map:a.name`. But the spec says dependency for Map key access is `data.map:a`; maybe they don't expect nested reads. To be robust, let's recurse
into map values too: when both maps have key k and values differ (objects), diff recursively under `data.map:a` to record deeper leaves, and also
record `data.map:a` itself (the key access). But then a selector depending only on `data.map:a` (just calls get without property access) would be
invalidated if the value object changes — correct, because the whole value changed. A selector depending on `data.map:a.name` would be invalidated
only if `.name` changed. Let's implement map diff recursively:
```ts
if (oldVal instanceof Map && newVal instanceof Map) {
  for (const k of keys) {
    if (!oldVal.has(k) || !newVal.has(k) || !Object.is(oldVal.get(k), newVal.get(k))) {
      res.push(joinPath(base, `map:${String(k)}`))
    }
    if (oldVal.has(k) && newVal.has(k) && !Object.is(oldVal.get(k), newVal.get(k)) && isObject(oldVal.get(k)) && isObject(newVal.get(k))) {
      res.push(...diffPaths(oldVal.get(k), newVal.get(k), joinPath(base, `map:${String(k)}`)))
    }
  }
}
```
Then version bumps for `data.map:a` (ancestor of `data.map:a.name`). Good.

Similarly for Sets, no recursion (membership only).

Now, **`joinPath`**:
```ts
function joinPath(base, key) {
  return base ? `${base}.${key}` : key
}
```

Now, **ancestor bump**:
```ts
function splitPath(path) {
  return path.split('.')
}
bumpPath(path) {
  const parts = splitPath(path)
  let cur = ''
  for (const p of parts) {
    cur = cur ? `${cur}.${p}` : p
    this.versions.set(cur, (this.versions.get(cur) ?? 0) + 1)
  }
}
```

Now, let's think about **the state diff for the logic slice** when logic path doesn't exist in old state (initial attach). `logic.selector(oldState)`
may throw because path missing. We'll guard in notify:
```ts
try {
  const prevRoot = this.logic.selector(prevState)
  const nextRoot = this.logic.selector(nextState)
  ...
} catch {}
```

Now, **when to notify**: The middleware should notify all engines **once** per action. We'll create a function `notifyAtomicEngines(prev, next)` in
the atomic module:
```ts
export function notifyAtomicEngines(prev, next) {
  if (prev === next) return
  const { atomicEngines } = getContext()
  for (const engine of atomicEngines) {
    engine.notify(prev, next)
  }
}
```

And `engine.notify`:
```ts
notify(prevState, nextState) {
  if (!this.mounted) return // or check context mount counter
  let prevRoot, nextRoot
  try {
    prevRoot = this.logic.selector(prevState)
    nextRoot = this.logic.selector(nextState)
  } catch { return }
  const changes = diffPaths(prevRoot, nextRoot, '')
  for (const p of changes) this.bumpPath(p)
}
```
Should we only notify mounted engines? If engine exists but logic not mounted, diffing could throw or produce irrelevant changes. We can check
`getContext().mount.counter[this.logic.pathString] > 0`. But engine for unmounted logic may later mount; versions from before mount are irrelevant.
Let's check mounted.

Actually, if the logic is built but not mounted, its reducer isn't attached, so state slice may not exist. Skipping is fine.

Now, let's also handle **`getAtomicEngine`**:
```ts
export function getAtomicEngine(logic) {
  const context = getContext()
  let engine = context.atomicEngines.get(logic)
  if (!engine) {
    engine = new AtomicSelectorEngine(logic)
    context.atomicEngines.set(logic, engine)
    context.atomicEnginesList.add(engine)
  }
  return engine
}
```

Now, let's implement. First, write `src/core/atomic-selectors.ts`.

Let me draft it carefully.

```ts
import { BuiltLogic, Logic, Selector } from '../types'
import { getContext } from '../kea/context'

type DepKind = 'selector' | 'state'

interface AtomicSelectorMeta {
  name: string
  args: Selector[]
  func: (...args: any[]) => any
  dependencies: Set<string>
  depKinds: Map<string, DepKind>
  lastDepVersions: Map<string, number>
  lastArgResults: any[]
  cached: any
  hasCached: boolean
  evaluations: number
  dirtyCause: string | null
  inProgress: boolean
}

class Session {
  engine: AtomicSelectorEngine
  meta: AtomicSelectorMeta
  stateDeps = new Set<string>()
  pendingDeps = new Map<string, { selector: string; whole: boolean; paths: Set<string> }>()
  rootRaw: any
  rootProxy: any

  constructor(engine, meta) { ... }

  getRoot(state) {
    if (this.rootProxy === undefined) {
      this.rootRaw = this.engine.logic.selector(state)
      this.rootProxy = createProxy(this.rootRaw, '', (p) => this.stateDeps.add(p))
    }
    return this.rootProxy
  }

  recordSelectorDep(name) {
    if (!this.pendingDeps.has(name)) {
      this.pendingDeps.set(name, { selector: name, whole: true, paths: new Set() })
    }
    return this.pendingDeps.get(name)!
  }

  wrapSelectorResult(name, value) {
    const entry = this.recordSelectorDep(name)
    if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
      return createProxy(value, name, (p) => { entry.whole = false; entry.paths.add(p) })
    }
    return value
  }
}
```

Wait: in `wrapSelectorResult`, when we create a proxy for the selector output, the recorder receives full paths like `user.name`. We add to
entry.paths. Good.

But there's a subtle bug: if a selector B calls A and gets a proxy, then **never accesses it** (e.g., `(user) => user` whole), the entry stays whole
-> dep `user`. If B accesses `user.name`, entry.whole=false, paths={'user.name'} -> dep `user.name`. Good.

Now, `AtomicSelectorEngine`:

```ts
class AtomicSelectorEngine {
  logic: BuiltLogic
  versions = new Map<string, number>()
  selectors = new Map<string, AtomicSelectorMeta>()
  currentSession: Session | null = null

  constructor(logic) { this.logic = logic }

  createReducerSelector(name) {
    this.selectors.set(name, { name, args: [], func: (root) => root[name], dependencies: new Set(), depKinds: new Map(), lastDepVersions: new Map(),
lastArgResults: [], cached: undefined, hasCached: false, evaluations: 0, dirtyCause: null, inProgress: false })
    return (state, props) => this.runReducerSelector(name, state, props)
  }

  createSelector(name, args, func) {
    this.selectors.set(name, { ... })
    return (state, props) => this.runSelector(name, state, props)
  }

  callSelectorFunction(name) {
    const engine = this
    const fn = (state, props) => engine.callSelector(name, state, props)
    ;(fn as any)._atomicSelectorName = name
    return fn
  }

  callSelector(name, state, props) {
    const session = this.currentSession
    const meta = this.selectors.get(name)
    if (!meta) {
      const value = this.logic.selectors[name](state, props)
      return session ? session.wrapSelectorResult(name, value) : value
    }
    const value = this.runSelector(name, state, props)
    return session ? session.wrapSelectorResult(name, value) : value
  }
```

Wait: for non-local selectors (connected), `session.wrapSelectorResult` records a whole dep and returns a proxy. But as discussed, versions for
connected deps won't bump. We'll handle via lastArgResults comparison. But also note: for non-local, if value is an object, returning a proxy allows
leaf refinement in health (deps like `connected.name`) but versions won't update, so B would never re-evaluate on `connected.name` changes unless the
whole object reference changes (lastArgResults comparison catches that). Good enough.

Now `runSelector`:

```ts
runSelector(name, state, props) {
  const meta = this.selectors.get(name)!
  if (meta.inProgress) throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
  const session = new Session(this, meta)
  const prevSession = this.currentSession
  this.currentSession = session
  meta.inProgress = true
  try {
    const argResults = meta.args.map((arg) => {
      const argName = (arg as any)._atomicSelectorName
      if (argName) {
        return this.callSelector(argName, state, props)
      }
      return arg(state, props)
    })

    const { dirty, cause } = this.checkDirty(meta)
    let finalDirty = dirty
    let finalCause = cause
    // non-local selector args comparison
    for (let i = 0; i < meta.args.length; i++) {
      const argName = (meta.args[i] as any)._atomicSelectorName
      if (argName && !this.selectors.has(argName)) {
        const current = argResults[i]
        const last = meta.lastArgResults[i]
        if (!Object.is(last, current)) {
          finalDirty = true
          if (!finalCause) finalCause = `selector:${argName}`
        }
      }
    }

    if (!finalDirty && meta.hasCached) {
      return meta.cached
    }

    const result = meta.func(...argResults)
    const rawResult = unwrap(result)
    this.commitDeps(meta, session)
    this.propagate(meta, meta.hasCached ? meta.cached : undefined, rawResult)
    meta.cached = rawResult
    meta.hasCached = true
    meta.evaluations += 1
    meta.dirtyCause = finalCause
    meta.lastArgResults = argResults.map(unwrap)
    // update lastDepVersions
    for (const d of meta.dependencies) {
      meta.lastDepVersions.set(d, this.versions.get(d) ?? 0)
    }
    return rawResult
  } finally {
    meta.inProgress = false
    this.currentSession = prevSession
  }
}
```

Wait, there's an issue with `session` and `callSelector`: `callSelector` for local meta calls `this.runSelector(name,...)`, which sets
`this.currentSession` to the callee's session, then restores to `prevSession` (which is the caller's session). Good. But in `callSelector`, after
`runSelector` returns, `session` (captured before) is the caller's session. We call `session.wrapSelectorResult`. Good.

But there's a problem: `argResults` are computed **before** `checkDirty`. If the selector is not dirty, we return `meta.cached` (raw), discarding
argResults. But we already may have caused A to re-evaluate and bump versions. That's fine. But note: we return `meta.cached` which is raw value;
callers (via callSelector) will wrap it in their session proxy. Good.

Now `runReducerSelector`:
```ts
runReducerSelector(name, state, props) {
  const meta = this.selectors.get(name)!
  if (meta.inProgress) throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
  const session = new Session(this, meta)
  const prevSession = this.currentSession
  this.currentSession = session
  meta.inProgress = true
  try {
    const { dirty, cause } = this.checkDirty(meta)
    if (!dirty && meta.hasCached) return meta.cached
    const rootProxy = session.getRoot(state)
    const result = rootProxy[name]
    const rawResult = unwrap(result)
    this.commitDeps(meta, session)
    this.propagate(meta, meta.hasCached ? meta.cached : undefined, rawResult)
    meta.cached = rawResult
    meta.hasCached = true
    meta.evaluations += 1
    meta.dirtyCause = cause
    for (const d of meta.dependencies) {
      meta.lastDepVersions.set(d, this.versions.get(d) ?? 0)
    }
    return rawResult
  } finally {
    meta.inProgress = false
    this.currentSession = prevSession
  }
}
```

Wait, there's a subtle issue: `session.getRoot(state)` uses `this.logic.selector(state)` which returns the raw root. Then `rootProxy[name]` triggers
recorder stateDep `name`. If the root is an object and `name` exists. If the reducer returns a default value and the root has `name` key. Good.

But what if `name` key doesn't exist in root (e.g., root is undefined because logic not attached)? Then `rootProxy[name]` returns undefined, no dep
recorded? The recorder is called in the proxy get trap regardless. Actually our object proxy get trap calls `recorder(childPath)` before/after getting
result, so it records `name` even if undefined. But if rootProxy is undefined (non-object), `createProxy` returns undefined, and `undefined[name]`
throws. But reducers only run when attached; fine.

Now `checkDirty`:
```ts
checkDirty(meta) {
  if (!meta.hasCached) return { dirty: true, cause: null }
  for (const d of meta.dependencies) {
    const current = this.versions.get(d) ?? 0
    const last = meta.lastDepVersions.get(d)
    if (last === undefined || current !== last) {
      const kind = meta.depKinds.get(d)
      return { dirty: true, cause: kind === 'selector' ? `selector:${d}` : d }
    }
  }
  return { dirty: false, cause: null }
}
```

Now `commitDeps`:
```ts
commitDeps(meta, session) {
  const dependencies = new Set<string>()
  const depKinds = new Map<string, DepKind>()
  for (const p of session.stateDeps) {
    dependencies.add(p)
    depKinds.set(p, 'state')
  }
  for (const entry of session.pendingDeps.values()) {
    if (entry.paths.size === 0) {
      dependencies.add(entry.selector)
      depKinds.set(entry.selector, 'selector')
    } else {
      for (const p of entry.paths) {
        dependencies.add(p)
        depKinds.set(p, 'state')
      }
    }
  }
  meta.dependencies = dependencies
  meta.depKinds = depKinds
}
```

Now `propagate`:
```ts
propagate(meta, oldVal, newVal) {
  if (Object.is(oldVal, newVal)) return
  const changes = diffPaths(oldVal, newVal, meta.name)
  for (const p of changes) this.bumpPath(p)
}
```
Note: if oldVal undefined (first run), `diffPaths(undefined, newVal, meta.name)` returns `[meta.name]` — bumps version of meta.name. That's fine (no
one depends on it yet maybe).

But there's a subtle issue: `propagate` bumps versions during evaluation. If a selector A is evaluated as part of B's arg evaluation, and A's version
bump makes B's checkDirty detect. Good. But if A's output changed and B is later evaluated, B sees bump. Good.

Now `notify`:
```ts
notify(prevState, nextState) {
  if (prevState === nextState) return
  const { mount } = getContext()
  if (!mount.counter[this.logic.pathString]) return
  let prevRoot, nextRoot
  try {
    prevRoot = this.logic.selector(prevState)
    nextRoot = this.logic.selector(nextState)
  } catch (e) { return }
  const changes = diffPaths(prevRoot, nextRoot, '')
  for (const p of changes) this.bumpPath(p)
}
```

Now `buildDependents` and `topologicalOrder` and `selectorHealth`.

Let's also think about **`lastArgResults` for local selectors**: We only compare non-local args. For local args, we rely on versions. But there's a
scenario: a local selector A's output changes (e.g., derived object) and B depends on whole A (`A`). A's propagate bumps version of `A` (because
diffPaths returns `A.name` etc. and bumpPath bumps ancestors including `A`). So B's dep `A` version changes. Good. If B depends on `A.name`, version
`A.name` changes. Good.

Now, let's also handle the case where a selector's deps include `A` as a **whole selector dep** but A is a root reducer selector and B uses the whole
object (e.g., `sameUser: (user) => user`). B's dep is `user` (kind selector). State change `user.age` bumps version `user` (ancestor), so B
re-evaluates. Good. If B reads `user.name`, dep `user.name` (kind state), state change `user.age` doesn't bump `user.name`, so B doesn't re-evaluate.
Good.

Now, **`buildDependents`** should include B in `user.dependents` when B's dep is `user` or `user.name`. Both first segment `user`. Good.

Now, **static circular detection** needs the recording proxy to record names. But our `selectorsProxy` get trap also returns callSelectorFunction.
We'll use the same proxy for static recording: in the atomic builder, we create a proxy whose get trap records the current selector name -> referenced
name, then returns `engine.callSelectorFunction(name)`. Then the args returned are callSelector functions. So a single `input(...)` call gives both
static deps and args.

Let's implement `atomicSelectorsBuilder` inside selectors.ts.

```ts
function atomicSelectorsBuilder(logic, selectorInputs) {
  const engine = getAtomicEngine(logic)
  const propSelectors = ... (same as current)
  const entries: { name: string; args: any[]; func: any }[] = []
  const staticDeps = new Map<string, Set<string>>()
  let currentName: string | null = null
  const selectorsProxy = new Proxy(logic.selectors, {
    get(target, prop, receiver) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      const name = String(prop)
      if (currentName) {
        if (!staticDeps.has(currentName)) staticDeps.set(currentName, new Set())
        staticDeps.get(currentName)!.add(name)
      }
      return engine.callSelectorFunction(name)
    },
  })

  for (const entry of Object.entries(selectorInputs)) {
    const [key, arr] = entry
    if (typeof logic.selectors[key] !== 'undefined') {
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Logic "${logic.pathString}" selector "${key}" already exists`)
    }
    if (!arr) throw ...
    const [input, func] = arr
    currentName = key
    const args = input(selectorsProxy, propSelectors)
    currentName = null
    if (args.filter((a) => typeof a !== 'function').length > 0) {
      const argTypes = args.map((a) => typeof a).join(', ')
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Logic "${logic.pathString}", selector "${key}" has incorrect input: [${argTypes}].`)
    }
    entries.push({ name: key, args, func })
  }

  detectCircular(staticDeps, logic)

  for (const { name, args, func } of entries) {
    addSelectorAndValue(logic, name, engine.createSelector(name, args, func))
  }
}
```

But wait: `engine.callSelectorFunction` is a method that references `this` (engine). The proxy returns the function; when called later, `this` is
irrelevant because it's an arrow? We'll define `callSelectorFunction` to return an arrow capturing engine. Fine.

Also, `input` may return args that include `propSelectors` proxy functions; those are plain functions, no marker. Good.

Now, let's consider **the order of creating selectors**: In current code, placeholders are added first so that `logic.selectors[key]` exists for all
keys before the second pass. In our atomic path, we don't add placeholders; we create all selectors in the final loop. During the final loop,
`engine.createSelector` sets `logic.selectors[name]`. If a selector's args reference a later selector via callSelectorFunction, it's fine because call
happens at runtime. Good.

Now, there's one more subtlety: `logic.selectors` may already contain **reducer selectors** created by `reducers()` (atomic wrappers) before
`selectors()` runs. The proxy getter for those returns `engine.callSelectorFunction(name)`, and `callSelector` will find meta in engine.selectors.
Good.

Now, let's also handle **connected values** in `connect.ts`. In atomic mode, connected values are added to `logic.selectors[to]` directly (plain or
other logic's atomic wrapper). They won't be in engine.selectors (unless to name collides with local — no). So `selectorHealth` won't include them.
That's acceptable.

But there's a subtlety: `connect.ts` defines `logic.selectors[to] = selector` and then defines `logic.values[to]` getter. If later `selectors()`
builder is called and the user defines a selector with the same name? It would throw "already exists". Fine.

Now, let's also think about **props change**. When logic props change, `getBuiltLogic` updates props and calls `propsChanged`. Selector results that
depend on props should re-evaluate. Our atomic engine's deps don't include props, so a prop-dependent selector would not be invalidated on props
change. In baseline Kea, reselect's parametric selectors re-evaluate when props change because `createSelector` uses props as an input and its
memoization compares props. Our engine currently doesn't handle props invalidation. The task doesn't explicitly mention props change, but "baseline
Kea behaviors remain unchanged" implies props changes should still work. We need to handle props.

How does React re-render on props change? The logic's `propsChanged` event fires, and `useMountedLogic` etc. In `useValues`, the getters call
`useSelector(builtLogic.selectors[key])`; `useSelector`'s getSnapshot calls `selector(getStoreState())` — but wait, `useSelector` only passes state,
not props! Let me check: `useSelector(selector)` in hooks.ts calls `selector(getStoreState())` with no props. So selector uses `logic.props` as
default. When props change, `logic.props` is updated by `getBuiltLogic`, and the next `getSnapshot` call would use new props. But
`useSyncExternalStore` only calls getSnapshot when the store notifies. If props change without a store change, React may not call getSnapshot.
However, in React, when a parent re-renders with new props, the component using `useValues` re-renders (because parent re-rendered), and `useSelector`
may be called again... Actually `useSyncExternalStore` caches snapshot; it calls getSnapshot during render if needed? The `useValues` memo depends on
`builtLogic.pathString`, not props. Hmm. This is existing behavior and tests pass. Let's not dig too deep; props handling in Kea is already somewhat
fragile.

But our atomic engine caches selector results keyed by state versions only, not props. If props change and the selector is not dirty by state
versions, it returns cached (old props result). This could break props-dependent selectors. To handle props, we can include props in the dirty check:
store a `propsVersion` or simply force all selectors dirty when `logic.props` object changes. The engine can track `lastProps` reference and, in
`checkDirty`, if `this.logic.props !== this.lastPropsRef`, mark dirty. But then all selectors re-evaluate on any props change, which is acceptable
(props are not fine-grained here). We can add a `propsVersion` counter bumped by a `propsChanged` hook.

Where to hook props changes? `getBuiltLogic` calls `cachedLogic.events.propsChanged` when props change. We can add a plugin event or hook into that.
Simplest: in `getBuiltLogic`, after updating props, if atomic enabled, call `getAtomicEngine(cachedLogic).notifyPropsChanged()`. But `getBuiltLogic`
is in `build.ts`; importing atomic module there is fine.

Alternatively, in the engine's `checkDirty`, compare `this.logic.props` reference to `meta.lastPropsRef` (stored per selector). If changed, dirty. But
`this.logic.props` is the same object reference mutated by Object.assign in `getBuiltLogic`; `lastPropsRef` would still point to the same object
(mutated), so reference comparison fails. We need a version counter.

Let's add `propsVersion` to engine, incremented in `notifyPropsChanged()`, and have each selector store `lastPropsVersion`. In `checkDirty`, if
`this.propsVersion !== meta.lastPropsVersion`, dirty. After computing, set `meta.lastPropsVersion = this.propsVersion`.

In `getBuiltLogic`, when props change (the `prevPropsClone` branch), call `getAtomicEngine(cachedLogic)?.notifyPropsChanged()` before/after events.

`getContext().options.atomicSelectors`.

ensures that when selector is next called, it re-evaluates with new props. That's an improvement.

detected at build time, so fine.

import { BuiltLogic, Selector } from '../types'
import { getContext } from '../kea/context'

type DepKind = 'selector' | 'state'

interface AtomicSelectorMeta {
  name: string
  args: Selector[]
  func: (...args: any[]) => any
  dependencies: Set<string>
  depKinds: Map<string, DepKind>
  lastDepVersions: Map<string, number>
  lastArgResults: any[]
  lastPropsVersion: number
  cached: any
  hasCached: boolean
  evaluations: number
  dirtyCause: string | null
  inProgress: boolean
}

interface PendingDep {
  selector: string
  whole: boolean
  paths: Set<string>
}

/** Tracks dependencies while a single selector is being evaluated. */
class Session {
  engine: AtomicSelectorEngine
  meta: AtomicSelectorMeta
  stateDeps = new Set<string>()
  pendingDeps = new Map<string, PendingDep>()
  rootRaw: any
  rootProxy: any

  constructor(engine: AtomicSelectorEngine, meta: AtomicSelectorMeta) {
    this.engine = engine
    this.meta = meta
  }

  getRoot(state: any): any {
    if (this.rootProxy === undefined) {
      this.rootRaw = this.engine.logic.selector(state)
      this.rootProxy = createProxy(this.rootRaw, '', (path: string) => this.stateDeps.add(path))
    }
    return this.rootProxy
  }

  recordSelectorDep(name: string): PendingDep {
    let entry = this.pendingDeps.get(name)
    if (!entry) {
      entry = { selector: name, whole: true, paths: new Set() }
      this.pendingDeps.set(name, entry)
    }
    return entry
  }

  wrapSelectorResult(name: string, value: any): any {
    const entry = this.recordSelectorDep(name)
    if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
      return createProxy(value, name, (path: string) => {
        entry.whole = false
        entry.paths.add(path)
      })
    }
    return value
  }
}

type Recorder = (path: string) => void

function createProxy(value: any, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') {
    return value
  }
  if (value instanceof Map) {
    return createMapProxy(value, basePath, recorder)
  }
  if (value instanceof Set) {
    return createSetProxy(value, basePath, recorder)
  }
  return createObjectProxy(value, basePath, recorder)
}

function createObjectProxy(value: any, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  return new Proxy(value, {
    get(target: any, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      }
      const key = String(prop)
      // Advanced array methods: `.includes()` checks every element.
      if (Array.isArray(target) && key === 'includes') {
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
          recorder(joinPath(basePath, String(i)))
        }
        return target.includes.bind(target)
      }
      // Reading the length depends on the whole collection.
      if (key === 'length') {
        recorder(basePath)
        return target.length
      }
      const result = Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      const childPath = joinPath(basePath, key)
      recorder(childPath)
      if (typeof result === 'object' && result !== null) {
        return createProxy(result, childPath, recorder)
      }
      return result
    },
    has(target: any, prop: string | symbol) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.has(target, prop)
      }
      recorder(joinPath(basePath, String(prop)))
      return Reflect.has(target, prop)
    },
  })
}

function createMapProxy(value: Map<any, any>, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  return new Proxy(value, {
    get(target: Map<any, any>, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      }
      if (prop === 'get' || prop === 'has') {
        return (...args: any[]) => {
          const key = args[0]
          const depPath = joinPath(basePath, `map:${String(key)}`)
          recorder(depPath)
          const result = target[prop](...args)
          if (prop === 'get' && typeof result === 'object' && result !== null) {
            return createProxy(result, depPath, recorder)
          }
          return result
        }
      }
      if (prop === 'size') {
        recorder(basePath)
        return target.size
      }
      return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
    },
  })
}

function createSetProxy(value: Set<any>, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  return new Proxy(value, {
    get(target: Set<any>, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      }
      if (prop === 'has') {
        return (...args: any[]) => {
          recorder(joinPath(basePath, `set:${String(args[0])}`))
          return target.has(...args)
        }
      }
      if (prop === 'size') {
        recorder(basePath)
        return target.size
      }
      return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
    },
  })
}

const proxyTargets = new WeakMap<object, any>()

function unwrap(value: any): any {
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && proxyTargets.has(value)) {
    return proxyTargets.get(value)
  }
  return value
}

// Register proxies so we can unwrap selector results that are proxies.
function trackProxy(proxy: object, target: any): void {
  proxyTargets.set(proxy, target)
}

// ---- patch createObjectProxy etc to register proxies ----
function registerProxy(proxy: object, target: any): any {
  trackProxy(proxy, target)
  return proxy
}

function joinPath(base: string, key: string): string {
  return base ? `${base}.${key}` : key
}

function isTrackableObject(value: any): boolean {
  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object'
}

function diffPaths(oldVal: any, newVal: any, base: string): string[] {
  if (Object.is(oldVal, newVal)) {
    return []
  }
  if (!isTrackableObject(oldVal) || !isTrackableObject(newVal)) {
    return [base]
  }
  if (oldVal instanceof Map && newVal instanceof Map) {
    const result: string[] = []
    const keys = new Set([...oldVal.keys(), ...newVal.keys()])
    for (const key of keys) {
      const keyPath = joinPath(base, `map:${String(key)}`)
      const oldHas = oldVal.has(key)
      const newHas = newVal.has(key)
      if (!oldHas || !newHas || !Object.is(oldVal.get(key), newVal.get(key))) {
        result.push(keyPath)
      }
      if (oldHas && newHas) {
        const oldValue = oldVal.get(key)
        const newValue = newVal.get(key)
        if (!Object.is(oldValue, newValue) && isTrackableObject(oldValue) && isTrackableObject(newValue)) {
          result.push(...diffPaths(oldValue, newValue, keyPath))
        }
      }
    }
    return result
  }
  if (oldVal instanceof Set && newVal instanceof Set) {
    const result: string[] = []
    for (const value of oldVal) {
      if (!newVal.has(value)) {
        result.push(joinPath(base, `set:${String(value)}`))
      }
    }
    for (const value of newVal) {
      if (!oldVal.has(value)) {
        result.push(joinPath(base, `set:${String(value)}`))
      }
    }
    return result
  }
  if (Array.isArray(oldVal) && Array.isArray(newVal)) {
    const result: string[] = []
    const length = Math.max(oldVal.length, newVal.length)
    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
      if (!Object.is(oldVal[i], newVal[i])) {
        const indexPath = joinPath(base, String(i))
        result.push(indexPath)
        if (isTrackableObject(oldVal[i]) && isTrackableObject(newVal[i])) {
          result.push(...diffPaths(oldVal[i], newVal[i], indexPath))
        }
      }
    }
    return result
  }
  // Plain objects
  const result: string[] = []
  const keys = new Set([...Object.keys(oldVal), ...Object.keys(newVal)])
  for (const key of keys) {
    const oldChild = oldVal[key]
    const newChild = newVal[key]
    if (!Object.is(oldChild, newChild)) {
      const childPath = joinPath(base, key)
      if (isTrackableObject(oldChild) && isTrackableObject(newChild)) {
        result.push(...diffPaths(oldChild, newChild, childPath))
      } else {
        result.push(childPath)
      }
    }
  }
  return result
}

function splitPath(path: string): string[] {
  return path.split('.')
}

export class AtomicSelectorEngine {
  logic: BuiltLogic
  versions = new Map<string, number>()
  selectors = new Map<string, AtomicSelectorMeta>()
  currentSession: Session | null = null
  propsVersion = 0

  constructor(logic: BuiltLogic) {
    this.logic = logic
  }

  createReducerSelector(name: string): Selector {
    const meta: AtomicSelectorMeta = {
      name,
      args: [],
      func: (root: any) => root[name],
      dependencies: new Set(),
      depKinds: new Map(),
      lastDepVersions: new Map(),
      lastArgResults: [],
      lastPropsVersion: 0,
      cached: undefined,
      hasCached: false,
      evaluations: 0,
      dirtyCause: null,
      inProgress: false,
    }
    this.selectors.set(name, meta)
    return (state: any, props: any) => this.runReducerSelector(name, state, props)
  }

  createSelector(name: string, args: Selector[], func: (...args: any[]) => any): Selector {
    const meta: AtomicSelectorMeta = {
      name,
      args,
      func,
      dependencies: new Set(),
      depKinds: new Map(),
      lastDepVersions: new Map(),
      lastArgResults: [],
      lastPropsVersion: 0,
      cached: undefined,
      hasCached: false,
      evaluations: 0,
      dirtyCause: null,
      inProgress: false,
    }
    this.selectors.set(name, meta)
    return (state: any, props: any) => this.runSelector(name, state, props)
  }

  callSelectorFunction(name: string): Selector {
    const engine = this
    const fn = ((state: any, props: any) => engine.callSelector(name, state, props)) as Selector & {
      _atomicSelectorName: string
    }
    fn._atomicSelectorName = name
    return fn
  }

  callSelector(name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
    const session = this.currentSession
    const meta = this.selectors.get(name)
    if (!meta) {
      const value = this.logic.selectors[name](state, props)
      return session ? session.wrapSelectorResult(name, value) : value
    }
    const value = this.runSelector(name, state, props)
    return session ? session.wrapSelectorResult(name, value) : value
  }

  private runSelector(name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
    const meta = this.selectors.get(name)
    if (!meta) {
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Atomic selector "${name}" not found`)
    }
    if (meta.inProgress) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    const session = new Session(this, meta)
    const previousSession = this.currentSession
    this.currentSession = session
    meta.inProgress = true
    try {
      const argResults = meta.args.map((arg) => {
        const argName = (arg as any)._atomicSelectorName
        if (argName) {
          return this.callSelector(argName, state, props)
        }
        return arg(state, props)
      })

      const { dirty, cause } = this.checkDirty(meta)
      let finalDirty = dirty
      let finalCause = cause

      // Selectors that are not part of this engine (e.g. connected values) can not
      // be tracked leaf-by-leaf, so compare their whole result instead.
      for (let i = 0; i < meta.args.length; i++) {
        const argName = (meta.args[i] as any)._atomicSelectorName
        if (argName && !this.selectors.has(argName)) {
          if (!Object.is(meta.lastArgResults[i], argResults[i])) {
            finalDirty = true
            if (!finalCause) {
              finalCause = `selector:${argName}`
            }
          }
        }
      }

      if (!finalDirty && meta.hasCached) {
        return meta.cached
      }

      const result = meta.func(...argResults)
      const rawResult = unwrap(result)
      this.commitDeps(meta, session)
      this.propagate(meta, meta.hasCached ? meta.cached : undefined, rawResult)
      meta.cached = rawResult
      meta.hasCached = true
      meta.evaluations += 1
      meta.dirtyCause = finalCause
      meta.lastArgResults = argResults.map(unwrap)
      meta.lastPropsVersion = this.propsVersion
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        meta.lastDepVersions.set(dep, this.versions.get(dep) ?? 0)
      }
      return rawResult
    } finally {
      meta.inProgress = false
      this.currentSession = previousSession
    }
  }

  private runReducerSelector(name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
    const meta = this.selectors.get(name)
    if (!meta) {
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Atomic reducer selector "${name}" not found`)
    }
    if (meta.inProgress) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    const session = new Session(this, meta)
    const previousSession = this.currentSession
    this.currentSession = session
    meta.inProgress = true
    try {
      const { dirty, cause } = this.checkDirty(meta)
      if (!dirty && meta.hasCached) {
        return meta.cached
      }
      const rootProxy = session.getRoot(state)
      const result = rootProxy[name]
      const rawResult = unwrap(result)
      this.commitDeps(meta, session)
      this.propagate(meta, meta.hasCached ? meta.cached : undefined, rawResult)
      meta.cached = rawResult
      meta.hasCached = true
      meta.evaluations += 1
      meta.dirtyCause = cause
      meta.lastPropsVersion = this.propsVersion
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        meta.lastDepVersions.set(dep, this.versions.get(dep) ?? 0)
      }
      return rawResult
    } finally {
      meta.inProgress = false
      this.currentSession = previousSession
    }
  }

  private checkDirty(meta: AtomicSelectorMeta): { dirty: boolean; cause: string | null } {
    if (!meta.hasCached || meta.lastPropsVersion !== this.propsVersion) {
      return { dirty: true, cause: null }
    }
    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
      const current = this.versions.get(dep) ?? 0
      const last = meta.lastDepVersions.get(dep)
      if (last === undefined || current !== last) {
        const kind = meta.depKinds.get(dep)
        return { dirty: true, cause: kind === 'selector' ? `selector:${dep}` : dep }
      }
    }
    return { dirty: false, cause: null }
  }

  private commitDeps(meta: AtomicSelectorMeta, session: Session): void {
    const dependencies = new Set<string>()
    const depKinds = new Map<string, DepKind>()
    for (const path of session.stateDeps) {
      dependencies.add(path)
      depKinds.set(path, 'state')
    }
    for (const entry of session.pendingDeps.values()) {
      if (entry.paths.size === 0) {
        dependencies.add(entry.selector)
        depKinds.set(entry.selector, 'selector')
      } else {
        for (const path of entry.paths) {
          dependencies.add(path)
          depKinds.set(path, 'state')
        }
      }
    }
    meta.dependencies = dependencies
    meta.depKinds = depKinds
  }

  private propagate(meta: AtomicSelectorMeta, oldVal: any, newVal: any): void {
    if (Object.is(oldVal, newVal)) {
      return
    }
    const changes = diffPaths(oldVal, newVal, meta.name)
    for (const change of changes) {
      this.bumpPath(change)
    }
  }

  private bumpPath(path: string): void {
    const parts = splitPath(path)
    let current = ''
    for (const part of parts) {
      current = current ? `${current}.${part}` : part
      this.versions.set(current, (this.versions.get(current) ?? 0) + 1)
    }
  }

  notify(previousState: any, nextState: any): void {
    if (previousState === nextState) {
      return
    }
    const { mount } = getContext()
    if (!mount.counter[this.logic.pathString]) {
      return
    }
    let previousRoot: any
    let nextRoot: any
    try {
      previousRoot = this.logic.selector(previousState)
      nextRoot = this.logic.selector(nextState)
    } catch (e) {
      return
    }
    const changes = diffPaths(previousRoot, nextRoot, '')
    for (const change of changes) {
      this.bumpPath(change)
    }
  }

  notifyPropsChanged(): void {
    this.propsVersion += 1
  }

  private buildDependents(): Map<string, Set<string>> {
    const dependents = new Map<string, Set<string>>()
    for (const meta of this.selectors.values()) {
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        const root = firstSegment(dep)
        if (this.selectors.has(root)) {
          if (!dependents.has(root)) {
            dependents.set(root, new Set())
          }
          dependents.get(root)!.add(meta.name)
        }
      }
    }
    return dependents
  }

  topologicalOrder(): string[] {
    const names = [...this.selectors.keys()]
    const indegree = new Map<string, number>(names.map((name) => [name, 0]))
    const adjacency = new Map<string, string[]>(names.map((name) => [name, []]))
    for (const meta of this.selectors.values()) {
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        const root = firstSegment(dep)
        if (root !== meta.name && this.selectors.has(root)) {
          adjacency.get(root)!.push(meta.name)
          indegree.set(meta.name, (indegree.get(meta.name) ?? 0) + 1)
        }
      }
    }
    const queue = names.filter((name) => indegree.get(name) === 0).sort()
    const order: string[] = []
    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const current = queue.shift()!
      order.push(current)
      for (const next of adjacency.get(current)!) {
        indegree.set(next, (indegree.get(next) ?? 0) - 1)
        if (indegree.get(next) === 0) {
          queue.push(next)
        }
      }
    }
    if (order.length !== names.length) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    return order
  }

  selectorHealth(): {
    selectors: Record<
      string,
      {
        dependencies: string[]
        dependents: string[]
        evaluations: number
        dirtyCause: string | null
      }
    >
    topologicalOrder: string[]
  } {
    const dependents = this.buildDependents()
    const selectors: Record<
      string,
      {
        dependencies: string[]
        dependents: string[]
        evaluations: number
        dirtyCause: string | null
      }
    > = {}
    for (const [name, meta] of this.selectors) {
      selectors[name] = {
        dependencies: [...meta.dependencies].sort(),
        dependents: [...(dependents.get(name) ?? [])].sort(),
        evaluations: meta.evaluations,
        dirtyCause: meta.dirtyCause,
      }
    }
    return { selectors, topologicalOrder: this.topologicalOrder() }
  }
}

function firstSegment(path: string): string {
  const index = path.indexOf('.')
  return index === -1 ? path : path.slice(0, index)
}

export function getAtomicEngine(logic: BuiltLogic): AtomicSelectorEngine {
  const context = getContext()
  let engine = context.atomicEngines.get(logic) as AtomicSelectorEngine | undefined
  if (!engine) {
    engine = new AtomicSelectorEngine(logic)
    context.atomicEngines.set(logic, engine)
  }
  return engine
}

export function notifyAtomicEngines(previousState: any, nextState: any): void {
  if (previousState === nextState) {
    return
  }
  const context = getContext()
  for (const engine of context.atomicEngines.values()) {
    engine.notify(previousState, nextState)
  }
}

export function detectCircularSelectorDependencies(
  dependencies: Map<string, Set<string>>,
): void {
  const state = new Map<string, 0 | 1 | 2>()
  const visit = (name: string) => {
    const current = state.get(name)
    if (current === 1) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    if (current === 2) {
      return
    }
    state.set(name, 1)
    const deps = dependencies.get(name)
    if (deps) {
      for (const dep of deps) {
        if (dependencies.has(dep)) {
          visit(dep)
        }
      }
    }
    state.set(name, 2)
  }
  for (const name of dependencies.keys()) {
    visit(name)
  }
}

// Patch the proxy factories to register proxies for unwrapping.
const originalObjectProxy = createObjectProxy
const originalMapProxy = createMapProxy
const originalSetProxy = createSetProxy

// Re-create with registration. We'll just redefine below.
function createObjectProxyWithTracking(value: any, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  const proxy = new Proxy(value, {
    get(target: any, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      }
      const key = String(prop)
      if (Array.isArray(target) && key === 'includes') {
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
          recorder(joinPath(basePath, String(i)))
        }
        return target.includes.bind(target)
      }
      if (key === 'length') {
        recorder(basePath)
        return target.length
      }
      const result = Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      const childPath = joinPath(basePath, key)
      recorder(childPath)
      if (typeof result === 'object' && result !== null) {
        return createProxy(result, childPath, recorder)
      }
      return result
    },
    has(target: any, prop: string | symbol) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.has(target, prop)
      }
      recorder(joinPath(basePath, String(prop)))
      return Reflect.has(target, prop)
    },
  })
  registerProxy(proxy, value)
  return proxy
}

function createMapProxyWithTracking(value: Map<any, any>, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  const proxy = new Proxy(value, {
    get(target: Map<any, any>, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      }
      if (prop === 'get' || prop === 'has') {
        return (...args: any[]) => {
          const key = args[0]
          const depPath = joinPath(basePath, `map:${String(key)}`)
          recorder(depPath)
          const result = target[prop](...args)
          if (prop === 'get' && typeof result === 'object' && result !== null) {
            return createProxy(result, depPath, recorder)
          }
          return result
        }
      }
      if (prop === 'size') {
        recorder(basePath)
        return target.size
      }
      return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
    },
  })
  registerProxy(proxy, value)
  return proxy
}

function createSetProxyWithTracking(value: Set<any>, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  const proxy = new Proxy(value, {
    get(target: Set<any>, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      }
      if (prop === 'has') {
        return (...args: any[]) => {
          recorder(joinPath(basePath, `set:${String(args[0])}`))
          return target.has(...args)
        }
      }
      if (prop === 'size') {
        recorder(basePath)
        return target.size
      }
      return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
    },
  })
  registerProxy(proxy, value)
  return proxy
}

// Overwrite createProxy to use tracking factories and register proxies.
const originalCreateProxy = createProxy
function createProxyWithTracking(value: any, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') {
    return value
  }
  if (value instanceof Map) {
    return createMapProxyWithTracking(value, basePath, recorder)
  }
  if (value instanceof Set) {
    return createSetProxyWithTracking(value, basePath, recorder)
  }
  return createObjectProxyWithTracking(value, basePath, recorder)
}

import { BuiltLogic, Selector } from '../types'
import { getContext } from '../kea/context'

type DepKind = 'selector' | 'state'

interface AtomicSelectorMeta {
  name: string
  args: Selector[]
  func: (...args: any[]) => any
  dependencies: Set<string>
  depKinds: Map<string, DepKind>
  lastDepVersions: Map<string, number>
  lastArgResults: any[]
  lastPropsVersion: number
  cached: any
  hasCached: boolean
  evaluations: number
  dirtyCause: string | null
  inProgress: boolean
}

interface PendingDep {
  selector: string
  whole: boolean
  paths: Set<string>
}

type Recorder = (path: string) => void

const proxyTargets = new WeakMap<object, any>()

function joinPath(base: string, key: string): string {
  return base ? `${base}.${key}` : key
}

function isTrackableObject(value: any): boolean {
  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object'
}

function registerProxy(proxy: object, target: any): any {
  proxyTargets.set(proxy, target)
  return proxy
}

function unwrap(value: any): any {
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && proxyTargets.has(value)) {
    return proxyTargets.get(value)
  }
  return value
}

function createObjectProxy(value: any, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  const proxy = new Proxy(value, {
    get(target: any, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      }
      const key = String(prop)
      // Advanced array methods: `.includes()` checks every element.
      if (Array.isArray(target) && key === 'includes') {
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
          recorder(joinPath(basePath, String(i)))
        }
        return target.includes.bind(target)
      }
      // Reading the length depends on the whole collection.
      if (key === 'length') {
        recorder(basePath)
        return target.length
      }
      const result = Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      const childPath = joinPath(basePath, key)
      recorder(childPath)
      if (isTrackableObject(result)) {
        return createProxy(result, childPath, recorder)
      }
      return result
    },
    has(target: any, prop: string | symbol) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.has(target, prop)
      }
      recorder(joinPath(basePath, String(prop)))
      return Reflect.has(target, prop)
    },
  })
  return registerProxy(proxy, value)
}

function createMapProxy(value: Map<any, any>, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  const proxy = new Proxy(value, {
    get(target: Map<any, any>, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      }
      if (prop === 'get' || prop === 'has') {
        return (...args: any[]) => {
          const key = args[0]
          const depPath = joinPath(basePath, `map:${String(key)}`)
          recorder(depPath)
          const result = target[prop](...args)
          if (prop === 'get' && isTrackableObject(result)) {
            return createProxy(result, depPath, recorder)
          }
          return result
        }
      }
      if (prop === 'size') {
        recorder(basePath)
        return target.size
      }
      return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
    },
  })
  return registerProxy(proxy, value)
}

function createSetProxy(value: Set<any>, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  const proxy = new Proxy(value, {
    get(target: Set<any>, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any) {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
      }
      if (prop === 'has') {
        return (...args: any[]) => {
          recorder(joinPath(basePath, `set:${String(args[0])}`))
          return target.has(...args)
        }
      }
      if (prop === 'size') {
        recorder(basePath)
        return target.size
      }
      return Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver)
    },
  })
  return registerProxy(proxy, value)
}

function createProxy(value: any, basePath: string, recorder: Recorder): any {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') {
    return value
  }
  if (value instanceof Map) {
    return createMapProxy(value, basePath, recorder)
  }
  if (value instanceof Set) {
    return createSetProxy(value, basePath, recorder)
  }
  return createObjectProxy(value, basePath, recorder)
}

/** Tracks dependencies while a single selector is being evaluated. */
class Session {
  engine: AtomicSelectorEngine
  meta: AtomicSelectorMeta
  stateDeps = new Set<string>()
  pendingDeps = new Map<string, PendingDep>()
  rootRaw: any
  rootProxy: any

  constructor(engine: AtomicSelectorEngine, meta: AtomicSelectorMeta) {
    this.engine = engine
    this.meta = meta
  }

  getRoot(state: any): any {
    if (this.rootProxy === undefined) {
      this.rootRaw = this.engine.logic.selector(state)
      this.rootProxy = createProxy(this.rootRaw, '', (path: string) => this.stateDeps.add(path))
    }
    return this.rootProxy
  }

  recordSelectorDep(name: string): PendingDep {
    let entry = this.pendingDeps.get(name)
    if (!entry) {
      entry = { selector: name, whole: true, paths: new Set() }
      this.pendingDeps.set(name, entry)
    }
    return entry
  }

  wrapSelectorResult(name: string, value: any): any {
    const entry = this.recordSelectorDep(name)
    if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
      return createProxy(value, name, (path: string) => {
        entry.whole = false
        entry.paths.add(path)
      })
    }
    return value
  }
}

function diffPaths(oldVal: any, newVal: any, base: string): string[] {
  if (Object.is(oldVal, newVal)) {
    return []
  }
  if (!isTrackableObject(oldVal) || !isTrackableObject(newVal)) {
    return [base]
  }
  if (oldVal instanceof Map && newVal instanceof Map) {
    const result: string[] = []
    const keys = new Set([...oldVal.keys(), ...newVal.keys()])
    for (const key of keys) {
      const keyPath = joinPath(base, `map:${String(key)}`)
      const oldHas = oldVal.has(key)
      const newHas = newVal.has(key)
      if (!oldHas || !newHas || !Object.is(oldVal.get(key), newVal.get(key))) {
        result.push(keyPath)
      }
      if (oldHas && newHas) {
        const oldValue = oldVal.get(key)
        const newValue = newVal.get(key)
        if (!Object.is(oldValue, newValue) && isTrackableObject(oldValue) && isTrackableObject(newValue)) {
          result.push(...diffPaths(oldValue, newValue, keyPath))
        }
      }
    }
    return result
  }
  if (oldVal instanceof Set && newVal instanceof Set) {
    const result: string[] = []
    for (const value of oldVal) {
      if (!newVal.has(value)) {
        result.push(joinPath(base, `set:${String(value)}`))
      }
    }
    for (const value of newVal) {
      if (!oldVal.has(value)) {
        result.push(joinPath(base, `set:${String(value)}`))
      }
    }
    return result
  }
  if (Array.isArray(oldVal) && Array.isArray(newVal)) {
    const result: string[] = []
    const length = Math.max(oldVal.length, newVal.length)
    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
      if (!Object.is(oldVal[i], newVal[i])) {
        const indexPath = joinPath(base, String(i))
        result.push(indexPath)
        if (isTrackableObject(oldVal[i]) && isTrackableObject(newVal[i])) {
          result.push(...diffPaths(oldVal[i], newVal[i], indexPath))
        }
      }
    }
    return result
  }
  // Plain objects
  const result: string[] = []
  const keys = new Set([...Object.keys(oldVal), ...Object.keys(newVal)])
  for (const key of keys) {
    const oldChild = oldVal[key]
    const newChild = newVal[key]
    if (!Object.is(oldChild, newChild)) {
      const childPath = joinPath(base, key)
      if (isTrackableObject(oldChild) && isTrackableObject(newChild)) {
        result.push(...diffPaths(oldChild, newChild, childPath))
      } else {
        result.push(childPath)
      }
    }
  }
  return result
}

function splitPath(path: string): string[] {
  return path.split('.')
}

function firstSegment(path: string): string {
  const index = path.indexOf('.')
  return index === -1 ? path : path.slice(0, index)
}

export class AtomicSelectorEngine {
  logic: BuiltLogic
  versions = new Map<string, number>()
  selectors = new Map<string, AtomicSelectorMeta>()
  currentSession: Session | null = null
  propsVersion = 0

  constructor(logic: BuiltLogic) {
    this.logic = logic
  }

  createReducerSelector(name: string): Selector {
    const meta: AtomicSelectorMeta = {
      name,
      args: [],
      func: (root: any) => root[name],
      dependencies: new Set(),
      depKinds: new Map(),
      lastDepVersions: new Map(),
      lastArgResults: [],
      lastPropsVersion: 0,
      cached: undefined,
      hasCached: false,
      evaluations: 0,
      dirtyCause: null,
      inProgress: false,
    }
    this.selectors.set(name, meta)
    return (state: any, props: any) => this.runReducerSelector(name, state, props)
  }

  createSelector(name: string, args: Selector[], func: (...args: any[]) => any): Selector {
    const meta: AtomicSelectorMeta = {
      name,
      args,
      func,
      dependencies: new Set(),
      depKinds: new Map(),
      lastDepVersions: new Map(),
      lastArgResults: [],
      lastPropsVersion: 0,
      cached: undefined,
      hasCached: false,
      evaluations: 0,
      dirtyCause: null,
      inProgress: false,
    }
    this.selectors.set(name, meta)
    return (state: any, props: any) => this.runSelector(name, state, props)
  }

  callSelectorFunction(name: string): Selector {
    const engine = this
    const fn = ((state: any, props: any) => engine.callSelector(name, state, props)) as Selector & {
      _atomicSelectorName: string
    }
    fn._atomicSelectorName = name
    return fn
  }

  callSelector(name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
    const session = this.currentSession
    const meta = this.selectors.get(name)
    if (!meta) {
      const value = this.logic.selectors[name](state, props)
      return session ? session.wrapSelectorResult(name, value) : value
    }
    const value = this.runSelector(name, state, props)
    return session ? session.wrapSelectorResult(name, value) : value
  }

  private runSelector(name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
    const meta = this.selectors.get(name)
    if (!meta) {
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Atomic selector "${name}" not found`)
    }
    if (meta.inProgress) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    const session = new Session(this, meta)
    const previousSession = this.currentSession
    this.currentSession = session
    meta.inProgress = true
    try {
      const argResults = meta.args.map((arg) => {
        const argName = (arg as any)._atomicSelectorName
        if (argName) {
          return this.callSelector(argName, state, props)
        }
        return arg(state, props)
      })

      const { dirty, cause } = this.checkDirty(meta)
      let finalDirty = dirty
      let finalCause = cause

      // Selectors that are not part of this engine (e.g. connected values) can not
      // be tracked leaf-by-leaf, so compare their whole result instead.
      for (let i = 0; i < meta.args.length; i++) {
        const argName = (meta.args[i] as any)._atomicSelectorName
        if (argName && !this.selectors.has(argName)) {
          if (!Object.is(meta.lastArgResults[i], argResults[i])) {
            finalDirty = true
            if (!finalCause) {
              finalCause = `selector:${argName}`
            }
          }
        }
      }

      if (!finalDirty && meta.hasCached) {
        return meta.cached
      }

      const result = meta.func(...argResults)
      const rawResult = unwrap(result)
      this.commitDeps(meta, session)
      this.propagate(meta, meta.hasCached ? meta.cached : undefined, rawResult)
      meta.cached = rawResult
      meta.hasCached = true
      meta.evaluations += 1
      meta.dirtyCause = finalCause
      meta.lastArgResults = argResults.map(unwrap)
      meta.lastPropsVersion = this.propsVersion
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        meta.lastDepVersions.set(dep, this.versions.get(dep) ?? 0)
      }
      return rawResult
    } finally {
      meta.inProgress = false
      this.currentSession = previousSession
    }
  }

  private runReducerSelector(name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
    const meta = this.selectors.get(name)
    if (!meta) {
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Atomic reducer selector "${name}" not found`)
    }
    if (meta.inProgress) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    const session = new Session(this, meta)
    const previousSession = this.currentSession
    this.currentSession = session
    meta.inProgress = true
    try {
      const { dirty, cause } = this.checkDirty(meta)
      if (!dirty && meta.hasCached) {
        return meta.cached
      }
      const rootProxy = session.getRoot(state)
      const result = rootProxy[name]
      const rawResult = unwrap(result)
      this.commitDeps(meta, session)
      this.propagate(meta, meta.hasCached ? meta.cached : undefined, rawResult)
      meta.cached = rawResult
      meta.hasCached = true
      meta.evaluations += 1
      meta.dirtyCause = cause
      meta.lastPropsVersion = this.propsVersion
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        meta.lastDepVersions.set(dep, this.versions.get(dep) ?? 0)
      }
      return rawResult
    } finally {
      meta.inProgress = false
      this.currentSession = previousSession
    }
  }

  private checkDirty(meta: AtomicSelectorMeta): { dirty: boolean; cause: string | null } {
    if (!meta.hasCached || meta.lastPropsVersion !== this.propsVersion) {
      return { dirty: true, cause: null }
    }
    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
      const current = this.versions.get(dep) ?? 0
      const last = meta.lastDepVersions.get(dep)
      if (last === undefined || current !== last) {
        const kind = meta.depKinds.get(dep)
        return { dirty: true, cause: kind === 'selector' ? `selector:${dep}` : dep }
      }
    }
    return { dirty: false, cause: null }
  }

  private commitDeps(meta: AtomicSelectorMeta, session: Session): void {
    const dependencies = new Set<string>()
    const depKinds = new Map<string, DepKind>()
    for (const path of session.stateDeps) {
      dependencies.add(path)
      depKinds.set(path, 'state')
    }
    for (const entry of session.pendingDeps.values()) {
      if (entry.paths.size === 0) {
        dependencies.add(entry.selector)
        depKinds.set(entry.selector, 'selector')
      } else {
        for (const path of entry.paths) {
          dependencies.add(path)
          depKinds.set(path, 'state')
        }
      }
    }
    meta.dependencies = dependencies
    meta.depKinds = depKinds
  }

  private propagate(meta: AtomicSelectorMeta, oldVal: any, newVal: any): void {
    if (Object.is(oldVal, newVal)) {
      return
    }
    const changes = diffPaths(oldVal, newVal, meta.name)
    for (const change of changes) {
      this.bumpPath(change)
    }
  }

  private bumpPath(path: string): void {
    const parts = splitPath(path)
    let current = ''
    for (const part of parts) {
      current = current ? `${current}.${part}` : part
      this.versions.set(current, (this.versions.get(current) ?? 0) + 1)
    }
  }

  notify(previousState: any, nextState: any): void {
    if (previousState === nextState) {
      return
    }
    const { mount } = getContext()
    if (!mount.counter[this.logic.pathString]) {
      return
    }
    let previousRoot: any
    let nextRoot: any
    try {
      previousRoot = this.logic.selector(previousState)
      nextRoot = this.logic.selector(nextState)
    } catch (e) {
      return
    }
    const changes = diffPaths(previousRoot, nextRoot, '')
    for (const change of changes) {
      this.bumpPath(change)
    }
  }

  notifyPropsChanged(): void {
    this.propsVersion += 1
  }

  private buildDependents(): Map<string, Set<string>> {
    const dependents = new Map<string, Set<string>>()
    for (const meta of this.selectors.values()) {
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        const root = firstSegment(dep)
        if (this.selectors.has(root)) {
          if (!dependents.has(root)) {
            dependents.set(root, new Set())
          }
          dependents.get(root)!.add(meta.name)
        }
      }
    }
    return dependents
  }

  topologicalOrder(): string[] {
    const names = [...this.selectors.keys()]
    const indegree = new Map<string, number>(names.map((name) => [name, 0]))
    const adjacency = new Map<string, string[]>(names.map((name) => [name, []]))
    for (const meta of this.selectors.values()) {
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        const root = firstSegment(dep)
        if (root !== meta.name && this.selectors.has(root)) {
          adjacency.get(root)!.push(meta.name)
          indegree.set(meta.name, (indegree.get(meta.name) ?? 0) + 1)
        }
      }
    }
    const queue = names.filter((name) => indegree.get(name) === 0).sort()
    const order: string[] = []
    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const current = queue.shift()!
      order.push(current)
      for (const next of adjacency.get(current)!) {
        indegree.set(next, (indegree.get(next) ?? 0) - 1)
        if (indegree.get(next) === 0) {
          queue.push(next)
        }
      }
    }
    if (order.length !== names.length) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    return order
  }

  selectorHealth(): {
    selectors: Record<
      string,
      {
        dependencies: string[]
        dependents: string[]
        evaluations: number
        dirtyCause: string | null
      }
    >
    topologicalOrder: string[]
  } {
    const dependents = this.buildDependents()
    const selectors: Record<
      string,
      {
        dependencies: string[]
        dependents: string[]
        evaluations: number
        dirtyCause: string | null
      }
    > = {}
    for (const [name, meta] of this.selectors) {
      selectors[name] = {
        dependencies: [...meta.dependencies].sort(),
        dependents: [...(dependents.get(name) ?? [])].sort(),
        evaluations: meta.evaluations,
        dirtyCause: meta.dirtyCause,
      }
    }
    return { selectors, topologicalOrder: this.topologicalOrder() }
  }
}

export function getAtomicEngine(logic: BuiltLogic): AtomicSelectorEngine {
  const context = getContext()
  let engine = context.atomicEngines.get(logic) as AtomicSelectorEngine | undefined
  if (!engine) {
    engine = new AtomicSelectorEngine(logic)
    context.atomicEngines.set(logic, engine)
  }
  return engine
}

export function notifyAtomicEngines(previousState: any, nextState: any): void {
  if (previousState === nextState) {
    return
  }
  const context = getContext()
  for (const engine of context.atomicEngines.values()) {
    engine.notify(previousState, nextState)
  }
}

export function detectCircularSelectorDependencies(dependencies: Map<string, Set<string>>): void {
  const state = new Map<string, 0 | 1 | 2>()
  const visit = (name: string) => {
    const current = state.get(name)
    if (current === 1) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    if (current === 2) {
      return
    }
    state.set(name, 1)
    const deps = dependencies.get(name)
    if (deps) {
      for (const dep of deps) {
        if (dependencies.has(dep)) {
          visit(dep)
        }
      }
    }
    state.set(name, 2)
  }
  for (const name of dependencies.keys()) {
    visit(name)
  }
}

    const propSelectors =
      typeof Proxy !== 'undefined'
        ? new Proxy(logic.props, {
            get(target, prop) {
              if (!(prop in target)) {
                throw new Error(
                  `[KEA] Prop "${String(prop)}" not found for logic "${
                    logic.pathString
                  }". Attempted to use in a selector. Please specify a default via props({ ${String(
                    prop,

                )

              return () => target[prop]
            },
          })
        : (Object.fromEntries(
            Object.keys(logic.props).map((key) => [key, () => logic.props[key]]),
          ) as LogicPropSelectors<L>)

    for (const entry of Object.entries(selectorInputs)) {
      const [key, arr]: [string, SelectorDefinition<L['selectors'], LogicPropSelectors<L>, any> | undefined] = entry
      if (!arr) {
        throw new Error(`[KEA] Logic "${logic.pathString}" selector "${key}" is undefined`)
      }
      const [input, func, memoizeOptions] = arr
      const args: ParametricSelector<any, any, any>[] = input(logic.selectors, propSelectors)

      if (args.filter((a) => typeof a !== 'function').length > 0) {
        const argTypes = args.map((a) => typeof a).join(', ')
        const msg = `[KEA] Logic "${logic.pathString}", selector "${key}" has incorrect input: [${argTypes}].`
        throw new Error(msg)
      }
      builtSelectors[key] = createSelector(args, func, { memoizeOptions })

      addSelectorAndValue(logic, key, (state = getStoreState(), props = logic.props) =>
        builtSelectors[key](state, props),
      )

      if (!logic.values.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
        Object.defineProperty(logic.values, key, {
          get: function () {
            return logic.selectors[key](getStoreState(), logic.props)
          },
          enumerable: true,
        })
      }
    }
  }
}

export function addSelectorAndValue<L extends Logic = Logic>(logic: L, key: string, selector: Selector): void {
  logic.selectors[key] = selector
  if (!logic.values.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
    Object.defineProperty(logic.values, key, {
      get: function () {
        return logic.selectors[key](getStoreState(), logic.props)
      },
      enumerable: true,
    })
  }
}

python to do the replacement.

Also, note that in the current code, `memoizeOptions` is passed to `createSelector` as `{ memoizeOptions }` — weird but existing. In atomic mode we
ignore memoizeOptions (custom memoization equality not supported; baseline behavior for custom memoization in atomic mode may differ, but that's
acceptable for this feature). Actually the selectors test has custom memoization. In atomic mode, if a test runs with atomic enabled and custom
memoization, it would not respect it. But the task doesn't require supporting custom memoization; default is fine. We'll ignore memoizeOptions.
